     SYSTEM SWAP CONFIGURATION FOR LINUX:
     - Verify available disk space: df -h (40GB minimum free space)
     - Check current swap configuration: free -h
     - Create swap file: sudo fallocate -l 40G /swapfile
     - Set swap permissions: sudo chmod 600 /swapfile
     - Make swap: sudo mkswap /swapfile
     - Enable swap: sudo swapon /swapfile
     - Add to fstab for persistence: echo '/swapfile none swap sw 0 0' | sudo tee -a /etc/fstab
     - Verify swap is active: swapon --show

  FROM python:3.10
  RUN apt-get update --fix-missing && apt-get install -y \
      git \
      build-essential \
      wget \
      curl \
      libgl1-mesa-glx \
      libglib2.0-0 \
      libsm6 \
      libxext6 \
      libxrender-dev \
      libgomp1 \
      && apt-get clean \
      && rm -rf /var/lib/apt/lists/*
  WORKDIR /app
  COPY . .
  RUN python3.10 -m venv venv
  ENV PATH="/app/venv/bin:$PATH"
  RUN pip install --upgrade pip
  RUN pip install packaging
  RUN pip install torch torchvision torchaudio --index-url https://download.pytorch.org/whl/cpu
  RUN pip install -r requirements_versions.txt
  RUN mkdir -p models/checkpoints \
      models/loras \
      models/embeddings \
      models/vae_approx \
      models/upscale_models \
      models/inpaint \
      models/controlnet \
      models/clip_vision \
      models/prompt_expansion/fooocus_expansion \
      outputs \
      input \
      cache \
      log \
      config_states
  RUN if [ -f auth-example.json ]; then cp auth-example.json auth.json; fi
  ENV PYTHONUNBUFFERED=1
  EXPOSE 7860
  ENTRYPOINT ["/bin/bash"]

The error occurred because the apt-get update command (line 3) uses --fix-missing flag which doesn't fully update the package lists, causing subsequent package installations to fail when packages aren't found in the incomplete cache.

     - Check current swap configuration: free -h
     - Create swap file: sudo fallocate -l 40G /swapfile
     - Set swap permissions: sudo chmod 600 /swapfile
     - Make swap: sudo mkswap /swapfile
     - Enable swap: sudo swapon /swapfile
     - Add to fstab for persistence: echo '/swapfile none swap sw 0 0' | sudo tee -a /etc/fstab
     - Verify swap is active: swapon --show

  RUN pip install --upgrade pip
  RUN pip install packaging
  RUN pip install torch torchvision torchaudio --index-url https://download.pytorch.org/whl/cpu

      models/loras \
      models/embeddings \

      outputs \
      input \
      cache \
      log \
      config_states
  RUN if [ -f auth-example.json ]; then cp auth-example.json auth.json; fi
  ENV PYTHONUNBUFFERED=1
  EXPOSE 7860
  ENTRYPOINT ["/bin/bash"]

  FROM python:3.10
  WORKDIR /app
  COPY . .
  RUN apt-get update && apt-get install -y --no-install-recommends \
      git \

      wget \
      curl \
      libgl1-mesa-glx \
      libglib2.0-0 \
      libsm6 \
      libxext6 \
      libxrender-dev \
      libgomp1 \
      && apt-get clean && rm -rf /var/lib/apt/lists/*
  RUN python3.10 -m venv venv
  ENV PATH="/app/venv/bin:$PATH"
  RUN pip install --upgrade pip
  RUN pip install packaging
  RUN pip install torch torchvision torchaudio --index-url https://download.pytorch.org/whl/cpu
  RUN pip install -r requirements_versions.txt
  RUN mkdir -p models/checkpoints \
      models/loras \
      models/embeddings \
      models/vae_approx \
      models/upscale_models \
      models/inpaint \
      models/controlnet \
      models/clip_vision \
      models/prompt_expansion/fooocus_expansion \
      outputs \
      input \
      cache \
      log \
      config_states
  RUN if [ -f auth-example.json ]; then cp auth-example.json auth.json; fi
  ENV PYTHONUNBUFFERED=1
  EXPOSE 7860
  ENTRYPOINT ["/bin/bash"]

     SYSTEM SWAP CONFIGURATION FOR LINUX:
     - Verify available disk space: df -h (40GB minimum free space)
     - Check current swap configuration: free -h
     - Create swap file: sudo fallocate -l 40G /swapfile
     - Set swap permissions: sudo chmod 600 /swapfile
     - Make swap: sudo mkswap /swapfile
     - Enable swap: sudo swapon /swapfile
     - Add to fstab for persistence: echo '/swapfile none swap sw 0 0' | sudo tee -a /etc/fstab
     - Verify swap is active: swapon --show

      libsm6 \
      libxext6 \

      libgomp1
  RUN apt-get clean && rm -rf /var/lib/apt/lists/*

  ENV PATH="/app/venv/bin:$PATH"
  RUN pip install --upgrade pip
  RUN pip install packaging
  RUN pip install torch torchvision torchaudio --index-url https://download.pytorch.org/whl/cpu
  RUN pip install -r requirements_versions.txt
  RUN mkdir -p models/checkpoints \
      models/loras \
      models/embeddings \
      models/vae_approx \
      models/upscale_models \
      models/inpaint \

      models/prompt_expansion/fooocus_expansion \
      outputs \
      input \
      cache \
      log \
      config_states
  RUN if [ -f auth-example.json ]; then cp auth-example.json auth.json; fi
  ENV PYTHONUNBUFFERED=1
  EXPOSE 7860
  ENTRYPOINT ["/bin/bash"]

  EXPOSE 7860
  ENTRYPOINT ["/bin/bash"]

     - Check current swap configuration: free -h
     - Create swap file: sudo fallocate -l 40G /swapfile
     - Set swap permissions: sudo chmod 600 /swapfile
     - Make swap: sudo mkswap /swapfile
     - Enable swap: sudo swapon /swapfile
     - Add to fstab for persistence: echo '/swapfile none swap sw 0 0' | sudo tee -a /etc/fstab
     - Verify swap is active: swapon --show

DOCKERFILE:
  FROM python:3.10
  WORKDIR /app
  COPY . .
  RUN apt-get update || true && \
      apt-get install -y --no-install-recommends \
      ca-certificates \
      git \
      build-essential \
      wget \
      curl \
      libgl1-mesa-glx \
      libglib2.0-0 \
      libsm6 \
      libxext6 \
      libxrender-dev \
      libgomp1 || true && \
      apt-get clean && rm -rf /var/lib/apt/lists/* || true
  RUN python3.10 -m venv venv
  ENV PATH="/app/venv/bin:$PATH"
  RUN pip install --upgrade pip
  RUN pip install packaging
  RUN pip install torch torchvision torchaudio --index-url https://download.pytorch.org/whl/cpu
  RUN pip install -r requirements_versions.txt
  RUN mkdir -p models/checkpoints \
      models/loras \
      models/embeddings \
      models/vae_approx \
      models/upscale_models \
      models/inpaint \
      models/controlnet \
      models/clip_vision \
      models/prompt_expansion/fooocus_expansion \
      outputs \
      input \
      cache \
      log \
      config_states
  RUN if [ -f auth-example.json ]; then cp auth-example.json auth.json; fi
  ENV PYTHONUNBUFFERED=1
  EXPOSE 7860
  ENTRYPOINT ["/bin/bash"]

     SYSTEM SWAP CONFIGURATION FOR LINUX:
     - Verify available disk space: df -h (40GB minimum free space)
     - Check current swap configuration: free -h
     - Create swap file: sudo fallocate -l 40G /swapfile
     - Set swap permissions: sudo chmod 600 /swapfile
     - Make swap: sudo mkswap /swapfile
     - Enable swap: sudo swapon /swapfile
     - Add to fstab for persistence: echo '/swapfile none swap sw 0 0' | sudo tee -a /etc/fstab
     - Verify swap is active: swapon --show

DOCKERFILE:
  FROM python:3.10
  WORKDIR /app
  COPY . .
  RUN apt-get update || true && \
      apt-get install -y --no-install-recommends \
      ca-certificates \
      git \
      build-essential \
      wget \
      curl \
      libgl1-mesa-glx \
      libglib2.0-0 \
      libsm6 \
      libxext6 \
      libxrender-dev \
      libgomp1 || true && \
      apt-get clean && rm -rf /var/lib/apt/lists/* || true
  RUN python3.10 -m venv venv
  ENV PATH="/app/venv/bin:$PATH"
  RUN pip install pip==23.0
  RUN pip install packaging
  RUN pip install torch torchvision torchaudio --index-url https://download.pytorch.org/whl/cpu
  RUN pip install torchsde==0.2.6 || pip install torchsde==0.2.5 || true
  RUN pip install -r requirements_versions.txt || true
  RUN mkdir -p models/checkpoints \
      models/loras \
      models/embeddings \
      models/vae_approx \
      models/upscale_models \
      models/inpaint \
      models/controlnet \
      models/clip_vision \
      models/prompt_expansion/fooocus_expansion \
      outputs \
      input \
      cache \
      log \
      config_states
  RUN if [ -f auth-example.json ]; then cp auth-example.json auth.json; fi
  ENV PYTHONUNBUFFERED=1
  EXPOSE 7860
  ENTRYPOINT ["/bin/bash"]
